==================== src\lib\islands.ts ====================
1|/**
2| * Island registry â€” single source of truth for every editable region the
3| * bridge can refresh. Each entry maps a URL slug under `/tina-island/...`
4| * to a fetcher + component + wrapper. Adding a new editable region = adding
5| * one entry here; the dynamic `[name].ts` route picks it up automatically.
6| */
7|import type { IslandRegistry } from '@tinacms/astro/experimental';
8|import type { QueryResult } from '@tinacms/astro/data';
9|
10|import type { BlogQuery, ConfigQuery, PageQuery } from '../../tina/__generated__/types';
11|import type { CmsBlog, CmsConfig, CmsPage } from './data';
12|import PageBody from '../components/islands/PageBody.astro';
13|import BlogBody from '../components/islands/BlogBody.astro';
14|import Header from '../templates/Header.astro';
15|import Footer from '../templates/Footer.astro';
16|import { getBlog, getConfig, getPage } from './data';
17|
18|export const islands: IslandRegistry = {
19|	page: {
20|		fetch: (_request, params) => getPage(params.get('slug') ?? 'home'),
21|		component: PageBody,
22|		wrapper: { tag: 'main' },
23|		propsFromData: (data) => ({
24|			data: (data as QueryResult<PageQuery>).data?.page as CmsPage | undefined,
25|		}),
26|	},
27|	blog: {
28|		fetch: (_request, params) => getBlog(params.get('slug') ?? ''),
29|		component: BlogBody,
30|		wrapper: { tag: 'article' },
31|		propsFromData: (data) => ({
32|			data: (data as QueryResult<BlogQuery>).data?.blog as CmsBlog | undefined,
33|		}),
34|	},
35|	global: {
36|		fetch: () => getConfig(),
37|		component: Header,
38|		wrapper: { tag: 'div' },
39|		propsFromData: (data) => ({
40|			config: (data as QueryResult<ConfigQuery>).data?.config as CmsConfig | undefined,
41|		}),
42|	},
43|	'global-footer': {
44|		fetch: () => getConfig(),
45|		component: Footer,
46|		wrapper: { tag: 'div' },
47|		propsFromData: (data) => ({
48|			config: (data as QueryResult<ConfigQuery>).data?.config as CmsConfig | undefined,
49|		}),
50|	},
51|};

==================== src\lib\data.ts ====================
1|/**
2| * Per-collection data loaders + the data shapes they return.
3| *
4| * Loaders call the generated Tina client and pipe the result through
5| * `requestWithMetadata()` so the editor overlay flows in when the page
6| * renders inside the admin iframe and `tinaField()` has its metadata.
7| *
8| * Types below are pure derivations — no hand-written shapes. Each one is
9| * either inferred from a loader's return type (`CmsConfig`/`CmsPage`/
10| * `CmsBlog`) or `Extract`/index-accessed off those. The Tina collection
11| * is the source of truth; regen with `tinacms dev` and everything
12| * downstream updates.
13| */
14|import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
15|import { join } from 'node:path';
16|import type { TinaRichTextContent } from '@tinacms/astro';
17|import { requestWithMetadata } from '@tinacms/astro/data';
18|import client from '../../tina/__generated__/client';
19|
20|function readFrontmatterValue(collection: 'blog' | 'page', slug?: string | null, key = 'permalink') {
21|	if (!slug) return null;
22|	try {
23|		const filePath = join(process.cwd(), 'src', 'content', collection, slug + '.mdx');
24|		const text = readFileSync(filePath, 'utf8');
25|		const match = text.match(/^---\r?\n([\s\S]*?)\r?\n---/);
26|		if (!match) return null;
27|		const lines = match[1].split(/\r?\n/);
28|		for (const line of lines) {
29|			const found = line.match(new RegExp('^' + key + '\\s*:\\s*(.*)$'));
30|			if (found) {
31|				let value = found[1].trim();
32|				if ((value.startsWith('"') && value.endsWith('"')) || (value.startsWith("'") && value.endsWith("'"))) {
33|					value = value.slice(1, -1);
34|				}
35|				return value || null;
36|			}
37|		}
38|	} catch (_error) {
39|		return null;
40|	}
41|	return null;
42|}
43|
44|function hydratePermalink<T extends { _sys?: { filename?: string | null } | null; permalink?: string | null }>(collection: 'blog' | 'page', node: T): T {
45|	const permalink = node.permalink || readFrontmatterValue(collection, node._sys?.filename || null, 'permalink');
46|	return permalink ? ({ ...node, permalink } as T) : node;
47|}
48|export const getConfig = () =>
49|	requestWithMetadata(client.queries.config({ relativePath: 'config.json' }));
50|
51|export const getHeaderNavigation = () =>
52|	requestWithMetadata(client.queries.navigation({ relativePath: 'header.json' }));
53|
54|export const getPage = (slug: string) =>
55|	requestWithMetadata(client.queries.page({ relativePath: `${slug}.mdx` }), { priority: 'primary' });
56|
57|export const getBlog = (slug: string) =>
58|	requestWithMetadata(client.queries.blog({ relativePath: `${slug}.mdx` }), { priority: 'primary' });
59|
60|export const getUser = (slug: string) =>
61|	requestWithMetadata(client.queries.user({ relativePath: `${slug}.json` }));
62|
63|export async function listPages() {
64|	const result = await client.queries.pageConnection();
65|	return (result.data.pageConnection.edges ?? [])
66|		.flatMap((edge) => (edge?.node ? [edge.node] : []))
67|		.map((node) => hydratePermalink('page', node));
68|}
69|
70|export async function listBlogs() {
71|	const result = await client.queries.blogConnection();
72|	return (result.data.blogConnection.edges ?? [])
73|		.flatMap((edge) => (edge?.node ? [edge.node] : []))
74|		.map((node) => hydratePermalink('blog', node))
75|		.sort((a, b) => {
76|			const ad = a.pubDate ? new Date(a.pubDate).valueOf() : 0;
77|			const bd = b.pubDate ? new Date(b.pubDate).valueOf() : 0;
78|			return bd - ad;
79|		});
80|}

85|export type CmsUser = Awaited<ReturnType<typeof getUser>>['data']['user'];

==================== tina\collections\page.ts ====================
1|import type { Collection } from 'tinacms';
2|import { heroBlockSchema } from '../../src/components/blocks/hero.template';

6|import { testimonialBlockSchema } from '../../src/components/blocks/testimonial.template';
7|import { calloutBlockSchema } from '../../src/components/blocks/callout.template';
8|import { contentBlockSchema } from '../../src/components/blocks/content.template';
9|import { videoBlockSchema } from '../../src/components/blocks/video.template';
10|import { splitBlockSchema } from '../../src/components/blocks/split.template';
11|import { aboutMockup17BlockSchema } from '../../src/components/blocks/about-mockup17.template';
12|import { seoFields } from '../fields/seo';
13|import { viewFrontendField } from '../fields/view-frontend';

15|function slugifyFilename(value?: string | null): string {
16|  if (!value || typeof value !== "string") return "untitled";
17|  let input = value.trim().toLowerCase();
18|  if (!input) return "untitled";
19|
20|  if (input.startsWith("http://") || input.startsWith("https://")) {
21|    const parts = input.split("/");
22|    input = parts.slice(3).join("/");
23|  }
24|
25|  input = input.split("?")[0].split("#")[0];
26|  input = input.split("\\").join("/");
27|  while (input.startsWith("/")) input = input.slice(1);
28|  while (input.endsWith("/")) input = input.slice(0, -1);
29|  if (input.startsWith("blog/")) input = input.slice(5);
30|
31|  let output = "";
32|  let lastWasDash = false;
33|  for (const ch of input) {
34|    const isLetter = ch >= "a" && ch <= "z";
35|    const isNumber = ch >= "0" && ch <= "9";
36|    const isSlash = ch === "/";
37|    const isAllowed = isLetter || isNumber || isSlash || ch === "-" || ch === "_" || ch === ".";
38|    if (isAllowed) {
39|      output += ch;
40|      lastWasDash = false;
41|    } else if (!lastWasDash) {
42|      output += "-";
43|      lastWasDash = true;
44|    }
45|  }
46|
47|  while (output.startsWith("-")) output = output.slice(1);
48|  while (output.endsWith("-")) output = output.slice(0, -1);
49|  while (output.includes("//")) output = output.split("//").join("/");
50|  return output || "untitled";
51|}
52|
53|function toPublicSlug(value?: string | null): string {
54|  if (!value || typeof value !== "string") return "";
55|  let input = value.trim().toLowerCase();
56|  if (!input) return "";
57|
58|  if (input.startsWith("http://") || input.startsWith("https://")) {
59|    const parts = input.split("/");
60|    input = parts.slice(3).join("/");
61|  }
62|  input = input.split("?")[0].split("#")[0];
63|  input = input.split("\\").join("/");
64|  while (input.startsWith("/")) input = input.slice(1);
65|  while (input.endsWith("/")) input = input.slice(0, -1);
66|  if (input.startsWith("blog/")) input = input.slice(5);
67|
68|  let out = "";
69|  let dash = false;
70|  for (const ch of input) {
71|    const ok = (ch >= "a" && ch <= "z") || (ch >= "0" && ch <= "9");
72|    if (ok) { out += ch; dash = false; }
73|    else if (!dash) { out += "-"; dash = true; }
74|  }
75|  while (out.startsWith("-")) out = out.slice(1);
76|  while (out.endsWith("-")) out = out.slice(0, -1);
77|  return out;
78|}
79|
80|export const PageCollection: Collection = {
81|  name: 'page',
82|  label: 'Pages',
83|  path: 'src/content/page',
84|  format: 'mdx',
85|  ui: {
86|    filename: {
87|      readonly: false,
88|      parse: (filename) => slugifyFilename(filename),
89|      slugify: (values) => slugifyFilename(values?.title || 'untitled'),
90|    },
91|    router: ({ document }) => {
92|      const slug = toPublicSlug(document?.permalink) || toPublicSlug(document?._sys?.filename);
93|      return slug ? "/" + slug : "/";
94|    },
95|  },
96|  fields: [
97|    viewFrontendField('page'),
98|    {
99|      name: 'title',
100|      label: 'Title',
101|      type: 'string',
102|      isTitle: true,
103|      required: true,
104|      description: 'Main visible page title shown at the top of the frontend page.',
105|    },
106|    {
107|      name: "permalink",
108|      label: "Filename / Permalink",
109|      type: "string",
110|      description: "Edit the public URL slug, similar to WordPress permalink. Example: my-custom-url. Leave blank to use the actual filename.",
111|    },
112|    seoFields,
113|    {
114|      type: 'object',
115|      list: true,
116|      name: 'blocks',
117|      label: 'Page Sections',
118|      description:
119|        "The visible content of the page. When the page starts with a Hero block, its Headline is the main on-page heading â€” edit that to change what visitors see at the top.",
120|      ui: { visualSelector: true },
121|      templates: [
122|        aboutMockup17BlockSchema,
